### app concurrent RSS delta 100 connections

Reports RSS growth across the concurrent request burst so sustained-load memory trends are visible in router benchmark output.

RSS delta rows are process-model sensitive; compare same-process adapters only and treat negative samples as contamination indicators.

Same-core mreact variant noise floor: +3.3% spread. Treat smaller cross-framework gaps in this case as inconclusive.

### app Cloudflare Worker request latency

Builds the Cloudflare Pages worker bundle and reports request latency through its exported fetch handler. A workerd/Miniflare harness should replace this fallback once the local workerd path-resolution failure is fixed.

This section currently compares mreact app-router variants only; it is not a cross-framework ranking.

Same-core mreact variant noise floor: +31.71% spread. Treat smaller cross-framework gaps in this case as inconclusive.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | **mreact-app-router** | app Cloudflare Worker request latency | 1.8743 | best | ms |
| 2 | **mreact-app-router+log enabled** | app Cloudflare Worker request latency | 1.8821 | +0.42% | ms |
| 3 | **mreact-app-router+mreact react-compat** | app Cloudflare Worker request latency | 2.4687 | +31.71% | ms |

### app server cold start

Measures production server cold-start latency when the adapter can isolate startup from build work.

This section currently compares mreact app-router variants only; it is not a cross-framework ranking.

Same-core mreact variant noise floor: +2.43% spread. Treat smaller cross-framework gaps in this case as inconclusive.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | **mreact-app-router+log enabled** | app server cold start | 229.1735 | best | ms |
| 2 | **mreact-app-router+mreact react-compat** | app server cold start | 229.7064 | +0.23% | ms |
| 3 | **mreact-app-router** | app server cold start | 234.7312 | +2.43% | ms |
